	* Eliminando as Operações Já Utilizadas Em Cotações Anteriores
	lcQuery = [Select a.EmpDopNums, a.CItens, a.Qtds ] + ;
			    [From SigCtTCt a ] + ;
			   [Where a.EmpDopNums = '] + lcEdn + [' And ] + ;
					 [a.CItens = ] + Str(LocalDados.CItens, 6) + [ ] + ;
			   [Order By a.EmpDopNums, a.CItens, a.Qtds]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalCotCt]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Processamento - LocalCotCt)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select LocalCotCt	
	Go Top In LocalCotCt
	If Not Eof([LocalCotCt])
		lnQtA = LocalDados.Qtds - LocalCotCt.Qtds
	EndIf

	If Not (lnQtA > 0)
		Delete In LocalDados
	EndIf
EndScan

Select LocalDados
Scan
	ldDat = Nvl(LocalDados.Datas, Ctot([]))
	lcEdn = LocalDados.Emps + LocalDados.Dopes + Str(LocalDados.Numes, 6)
	
	If Not Seek(Dtos(ldDat) + lcEdn, [crOperacoes], [Ordem])
		Insert Into crOperacoes (Emps, Dopes, Numes, Datas, EmpDopNums, Nops, ChkSubn, LCancelas, ChkBxParcs, ;
								 ChkPagos, ProcBals, ProcDBal, Destinos, EstDests, EstOrigs, Origems, Produc, Jobs) ;
						 Values (LocalDados.Emps, LocalDados.Dopes, LocalDados.Numes, ldDat, lcEdn, LocalDados.Nops, ;
						 		 LocalDados.ChkSubn, LocalDados.LCancelas, LocalDados.ChkBxParcs, LocalDados.ChkPagos, ;
						 		 LocalDados.ProcBals, LocalDados.ProcDBal, LocalDados.Destinos, LocalDados.EstDests, ;
						 		 LocalDados.EstOrigs, LocalDados.Origems, LocalDados.Produc, LocalDados.Jobs)
	EndIf
	Replace Itens With Itens + LocalDados.Qtds In crOperacoes
EndScan

Select crOperacoes
Go Top In crOperacoes

* Cores da Grade de Operações da Pasta Dados
With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.grdOperacoes
	If (crSigCdPam.Gesind = 1)
		If (crOperacoes.Produc <> 1)
			.SetAll([DynamicForeColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.LCancelas,Rgb(255,0,0),Iif(crOperacoes.ChkSubn,Rgb(0,0,255),Rgb(0,0,0)))],[Column])
			If (crOperacoes.Origems=1 And InList(crOperacoes.EstOrigs,1,2,4)) Or (crOperacoes.Destinos=1 And InList(crOperacoes.EstDests,1,2,4))
				.SetAll([DynamicBackColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.ProcBals And crOperacoes.ProcDBal,Rgb(220,255,220), ;
										    Iif(crOperacoes.ProcBals,Rgb(255,209,164),Iif(crOperacoes.ProcDBal,Rgb(255,255,196), ;
										    Iif(crOperacoes.ChkPagos,Rgb(160,243,231),Rgb(255,255,255)))))],[Column])
			EndIf
		Else
			.SetAll([DynamicForeColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.ChkSubn,Rgb(0,0,225),Iif(crOperacoes.ChkBxParcs,Rgb(255,0,255),Iif(crOperacoes.Nops>0,Rgb(255,0,0),Rgb(0,0,0))))],[Column])
			.SetAll([DynamicBackColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.Nops>0 And (crOperacoes.ChkSubn Or crOperacoes.ChkBxParcs),Rgb(249,249,0),Rgb(255,255,255))],[Column])
		EndIf
	Else
		.SetAll([DynamicBackColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.ChkPagos,Rgb(160,243,231),Rgb(255,255,255))],[Column])
		.SetAll([DynamicForeColor],[Iif(crOperacoes.LCancelas,Rgb(255,0,0),Iif(crOperacoes.ChkSubn,Rgb(0,0,255),Iif(crOperacoes.ChkBxParcs,Rgb(255,0,255),Rgb(0,0,0))))],[Column])
	EndIf
EndWith

oTop.SubTitulo.Caption = [Carregando as Cotações Pendentes]
oTop.Update(.t.)

lcQuery = [Select a.Situas, a.Codigos, a.DtSolics, a.Solicitas, a.Deptos, a.Prioris, a.DtCotas, ] + ;
				 [a.DtAprovs, a.Aprovador, a.ContaEs, a.LocalEnts, a.Justificas, a.Copias ] + ;
		    [From SigCtTDc a ] + ;
		   [Where a.Situas = 0 Or ] + ;
		   		 [(Not a.Situas = 0 And a.DtCotas BetWeen ?pDtI And ?pDtF) ] + ;
		   [Order By a.Codigos]

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalCotDc]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Processamento - LocalCotDc)])
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select LocalCotDc
Scan
	lcPri = Iif(LocalCotDc.Situas <> 0, [Ped.Gerado], Iif(LocalCotDc.Prioris = 3, [Alta], Iif(LocalCotDc.Prioris = 2, [Média], [Normal])))

	Insert Into crCotaPends (Situas, Codigos, DtSolics, Solicitas, Deptos, Prioris, DtCotas, DtAprovs, ;
							 Aprovador, ContaEs, LocalEnts, Justificas, Copias) ;
					 Values (LocalCotDc.Situas, LocalCotDc.Codigos, Nvl(LocalCotDc.DtSolics, Ctod([])), ;
					 		 LocalCotDc.Solicitas, LocalCotDc.Deptos, lcPri, Nvl(LocalCotDc.DtCotas, Ctod([])), ;
					 		 Nvl(LocalCotDc.DtAprovs, Ctod([])), LocalCotDc.Aprovador, LocalCotDc.ContaEs, ;
					 		 LocalCotDc.LocalEnts, Nvl(LocalCotDc.Justificas, []), LocalCotDc.Copias)
EndScan
Select crOperacoes

oTop.Complete

If (Reccount([crOperacoes]) < 1) And (Reccount([crCotaPends]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Não Foram Encontrados Dados Para Processar!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
	loFil.getDtPerI.SetFocus
	Return .f.
Else
	Select crCotaPends
	Go Top In crCotaPends

	loDad.cmdCotacoes.btnAlterar.Enabled = (crCotaPends.Situas = 0)
	loDad.cmdCotacoes.btnExcluir.Enabled = (crCotaPends.Situas = 0)
	loDad.cmdCopiar.btnCopiar.Enabled    = Not IsEmpty(crCotaPends.DtAprovs) And Not Empty(crCotaPends.Aprovador) And Empty(crCotaPends.Copias)

	Select crOperacoes
	Go Top In crOperacoes

	loFil.Enabled = .f.
	loDad.Enabled = .t.
	loCot.Enabled = .f.
	loPed.Enabled = .f.

	loDad.cmdProblemas.Visible = fChecaAcesso([SIGMVCOT], [COTSEMOPE])
	
	loDad.grdOperacoes.Refresh
	loDad.grdCotaPends.Refresh

	ThisForm.pgfPagina.ActivePage = 2
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.Refresh

	loDad.grdOperacoes.Column1.SetFocus
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE processaperiodo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE processaperiodo
Lparameters P1, P2, P3 && Rotina Criada Apenas Para Não Gerar Erro Nas Chamadas do SigMvCab.
Return .t.
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
Local loFil, loDad, loCot, loPed

loFil = ThisForm.PgfPagina.pgFiltros
loDad = ThisForm.PgfPagina.pgDados
loCot = ThisForm.PgfPagina.pgCotacoes
loPed = ThisForm.PgfPagina.pgPedidos

With ThisForm
	.poDataMgr = CreateObject([fSqlConector], .Name)
	.poDataNew = CreateObject([fSqlConector], .Name)

	If (.poDataMgr.pnIdconn > 0)
		With .poDataMgr
			.AddCursor([SigCtTDc], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTDc])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTFn], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTFn])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTCt], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTCt])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTPc], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTPc])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTPe], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTPe])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTPi], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTPi])
			.AddCursor([SigCtTPs], [cIdChaves], [crSigCtTPs])

			.AddCursor([SigMvCab], [cIdChaves], [crSigMvCab])
			.AddCursor([SigMvItn], [cIdChaves], [crSigMvItn])
			.AddCursor([SigMvIts], [cIdChaves], [crSigMvIts])
			.AddCursor([SigMvPar], [cIdChaves], [crSigMvPar])
			.AddCursor([SigMvNfi], [cIdChaves], [crSigMvNfi])
			.Addcursor([SigMvCcr], [CidChaves], [cqSigMvCcr])
			.Addcursor([SigMvCpv], [CidChaves], [crSigMvCpv])			
		EndWith

		.loObj = CreateObject([Relation])
		.loObj.AddProperty([DtPerI],    Date())
		.loObj.AddProperty([DtPerF],    Date())
		.loObj.AddProperty([Dopes],     [])
		.loObj.AddProperty([Emps],      [])
		.loObj.AddProperty([pcEscolha], [])
		.loObj.AddProperty([Consulta],  Null)
		.loObj.AddProperty([Copiando],  .f.)
		.loObj.AddProperty([LibSolics], .f.)
		.loObj.AddProperty([Jobs], [])

		.GrupoOper = []

		lcQuery = [Select a.Dopes, a.Ordes, Str(a.Ndopes, 16, 0) as NDopes ] + ;
					[From SigCdOpe a ] + ;
					[join SigOpCdd b on a.dopes = b.dopes ] + ;
					[where b.Cotacaos = 1 and b.CotReqGer = 1 ]
		If (.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crCotReq]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Init - crCotReq)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		Select crCotReq
		Index on Dopes Tag Dopes
		Scan
			Wait Window 'Filtrando operações - Número : ' + crCotReq.dopes Nowait
			If !fAcessoMovmto(Usuar, crCotReq.dopes)
				Delete
			Endif
		Endscan
		Go Top In crCotReq
		
		lcQuery = [Select a.Dopes, a.Ordes, Str(a.Ndopes, 16, 0) as NDopes ] + ;
					[From SigCdOpe a ] + ;
					[join SigOpCdd b on a.dopes = b.dopes ] + ;
					[where b.Cotacaos = 1 and b.CotReqGer = 2 ]
		If (.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crCotGer]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Init - crCotGer)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		Select crCotGer
		Index on Dopes Tag Dopes
		Scan
			Wait Window 'Filtrando operações - Número : ' + crCotGer.dopes Nowait
			If !fAcessoMovmto(Usuar, crCotGer.dopes)
				Delete
			Endif
		Endscan
		Go Top In crCotGer

		lcQuery = [Select Gesind, MascNums, MoedaPs From SigCdPam]
		If (.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [crSigCdPam]) < 1)
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (Init - crSigCdPam)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select crSigCdPam
		Go Top In crSigCdPam

		.MontaGrades
		
		=DoDefault()

		ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdEmp], [LocalEmp], [Cemps], _Empr, [Razas])
		If Not Used([LocalEmp])
			=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (CursorQuery - LocalEmp)])
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		loFil.getCEmps.Value  = _Empr
		loFil.getDEmps.Value  = LocalEmp.Razas
		loFil.getDtPerI.Value = Date()
		loFil.getDtPerF.Value = Date()

		loFil.Enabled = .t.
		loDad.Enabled = .f.
		loCot.Enabled = .f.
		loPed.Enabled = .f.

		llAce = fChecaAcesso([SIGMVCOT], [COPIAR])
		loDad.cmdCopiar.Visible = llAce

		.loObj.LibSolics = fChecaAcesso([SIGMVCOT], [LIBSOLICS])

		.PgfPagina.ActivePage = 1
		.PgfPagina.Refresh

		loFil.getDtPerI.SetFocus
	Else
		=Messagebox([Impossível Efetuar Conexão Com o Servidor de Banco de Dados...], 0+48, [CreateObject])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndWith
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Load
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Load
=fConfigGeral()

Create Cursor crOperacoes (Marcas l(1), Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Datas d(8), Itens n(6), EmpDopNums c(29), ;
						   Nops n(6), ChkSubn l(1), LCancelas l(1), ChkBxParcs l(1), ChkPagos l(1), ProcBals l(1), ;
						   ProcDBal l(1), Destinos n(1), EstDests n(1), EstOrigs n(1), Origems n(1), Produc n(1), Jobs c(10))
Index On Dtos(Datas) + EmpDopNums Tag Ordem

Create Cursor crCotaPends (Situas n(1), Codigos n(6), DtSolics d(8), Solicitas c(10), Deptos c(10), Prioris c(10), ;
						   DtCotas d(8), DtAprovs d(8), Aprovador c(10), ContaEs c(10), LocalEnts n(10), Justificas m(4), ;
						   Copias n(6))
Index On Codigos Tag Ordem

Create Cursor crPedidos (IFors c(10), Fornecs c(50), FPags c(12), DtEnts d(8), Cpros c(14), DPros c(40), CUnis c(3), ;
						 CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4), Obs m(4), Qtds n(12,2), Valors n(12,6), Fretes c(12), VlFretes n(12,2))
Index On IFors + CPros Tag Ordem

Create Cursor crCabOperas (Numes n(6), IFors c(10), Fornecs c(50), FPags c(12), DtEnts d(8), Qtds n(12,2), Valors n(12,6), ;
						   Fretes c(12), VlFretes n(12,2), Jobs c(10))
Index On IFors Tag IFors

Create Cursor crItnOperas (CItens n(6), Numes n(6), CPros c(14), DPros c(40), CUnis c(3), Obs m(4), Qtds n(12,2), ;
						   Units n(12,6), Valors n(12,6), CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4))
Index On Str(Numes, 6) + CPros + Str(Units, 12, 4) Tag Ordem

Create Cursor crCabecalho (Codigos n(6), Tipo c(20), Comprador c(10), NmComprador c(30), Deptos c(10), DtCotas d(8), ;
						   DtPagtos d(8), DtSolics d(8), DtValids d(8), Prioris c(12), Solicitas c(10), Grades c(1), ;
						   Justificas m(4), ContaEs c(10), EntRClis c(50), EntEndes c(80), EntCeps c(9), EntCidas c(30), ;
						   EntBairs c(40), EntEstas c(2), Aprovador c(10), NmAprovador c(30), AprSols c(10), NmAprSols c(30), ;
						   IFors01 c(10), Fornec01 c(50), Contato01 c(20), Tel1s01 c(20), Condicao01 c(12), DtEnts01 d(8), Fretes01 c(12), VlFretes01 n(12,2), ;
						   IFors02 c(10), Fornec02 c(50), Contato02 c(20), Tel1s02 c(20), Condicao02 c(12), DtEnts02 d(8), Fretes02 c(12), VlFretes02 n(12,2), ;
						   IFors03 c(10), Fornec03 c(50), Contato03 c(20), Tel1s03 c(20), Condicao03 c(12), DtEnts03 d(8), Fretes03 c(12), VlFretes03 n(12,2), ;
						   IFors04 c(10), Fornec04 c(50), Contato04 c(20), Tel1s04 c(20), Condicao04 c(12), DtEnts04 d(8), Fretes04 c(12), VlFretes04 n(12,2), ;
						   IFors05 c(10), Fornec05 c(50), Contato05 c(20), Tel1s05 c(20), Condicao05 c(12), DtEnts05 d(8), Fretes05 c(12), VlFretes05 n(12,2), ;
						   IFors06 c(10), Fornec06 c(50), Contato06 c(20), Tel1s06 c(20), Condicao06 c(12), DtEnts06 d(8), Fretes06 c(12), VlFretes06 n(12,2))

Create Cursor crDetalhe (CPros c(14), DPros c(40), Obs m(4), Qtds n(12,2), QtdAdqs n(12,2), CUnis c(3), DUnis c(8), ;
						 CodCors c(4), CodTams c(4), ValAdqs n(12,6), ;
					     Marca01 l(1), Unit01 n(12,6), Tota01 n(12,6), Marca02 l(1), Unit02 n(12,6), Tota02 n(12,6), ;
					     Marca03 l(1), Unit03 n(12,6), Tota03 n(12,6), Marca04 l(1), Unit04 n(12,6), Tota04 n(12,6), ;
					     Marca05 l(1), Unit05 n(12,6), Tota05 n(12,6), Marca06 l(1), Unit06 n(12,6), Tota06 n(12,6))

Create Cursor crFretes (Fretes c(12))
Insert Into crFretes (Fretes) Values ([Emitente])
Insert Into crFretes (Fretes) Values ([Destinatario])
Index On Fretes Tag Fretes

=DoDefault()
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

ThisForm.poDataNew.Release

=DoDefault()

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getDopes
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 9
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

Select crCotReq
If Reccount([crCotReq])>0
	If Not Empty(This.Value)
		If Not Seek(Padr(This.Value, 20), [crCotReq], [Dopes])
			loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaInt], [crCotReq], [Dopes], Padr(This.Value, 20), [Operações], .t.)
			loLista.mAddColuna([Dopes], [], [Operação])
			loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		If (Lastkey()=27)
			This.Value = []
		Else
			This.Value = crCotReq.Dopes
		EndIf
	EndIf
Else
	=fAcessoMovmto(Usuar, This.Value, This)
Endif
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.cmdBotoes
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 13
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnProcessar.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnProcessar.Click
Local loFil

loFil = ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros

* Checando a Consistência dos Dados Para Processamento
If Empty(loFil.getDtPerI.Value)
	=MessageBox([Data Inicial Inválida!!!], 48, [Dados Inválidos...])
	loFil.getDtPerI.SetFocus
	Return 0
EndIf

If Empty(loFil.getDtPerF.Value)
	=MessageBox([Data Final Inválida!!!], 48, [Dados Inválidos...])
	loFil.getDtPerF.SetFocus
	Return 0
EndIf

If (loFil.getDtPerI.Value > loFil.getDtPerF.Value)
	=MessageBox([Data Inicial Não Pode Ser Maior Que a Final!!!], 48, [Dados Inválidos...])
	loFil.getDtPerI.SetFocus
	Return 0
EndIf

If Empty(loFil.getDopes.Value)
	=MessageBox([Operação Inválida!!!], 48, [Dados Inválidos...])
	loFil.getDopes.SetFocus
	Return 0
EndIf

If (MessageBox([Confirma o Processamento?], 4+32+256, [Confirmação!!!]) = 6)
	ThisForm.loObj.DtPerI = loFil.getDtPerI.Value
	ThisForm.loObj.DtPerF = loFil.getDtPerF.Value
	ThisForm.loObj.Dopes  = loFil.getDopes.Value
	ThisForm.loObj.Emps   = loFil.getCEmps.Value

	ThisForm.Processamento
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
ThisForm.Release
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getDjob
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 15
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Grupo = ''
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If Not fAcessoContas(Usuar, Grupo, 'D', This.Value, ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getjob, ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getdjob, .f., .f. ,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,.f.,10)
		=Messagebox('Acesso Negado!!!', 0+48, '')
		This.Value = ''
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getjob.Value = ''
		Return .f.
	EndIf
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getjob.Value = ''
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Empty(ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgFiltros.getJob.Value)
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
Lparameters nColIndex

With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.cmdCotacoes
	.btnAlterar.Enabled = (crCotaPends.Situas = 0)
	.btnExcluir.Enabled = (crCotaPends.Situas = 0)

	If .Visible
		.btnCopiar.Enabled = Not IsEmpty(crCotaPends.DtAprovs) And Not Empty(crCotaPends.Aprovador) And ;
							 Not IsEmpty(crCotaPends.DtSolics) And Not Empty(crCotaPends.Solicitas) And ;
							 Empty(crCotaPends.Copias)
	EndIf
EndWith

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Column1.Check1
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 39
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
NoDefault
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LParameters nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

Do Case
	Case InList(nKeyCode, 13, 32)
		Replace Marcas With Not Marcas In crOperacoes
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Refresh 
		NoDefault

	Case InList(nKeyCode, 9) && TAB
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.cmdMarcaDesmarca.btnMarcar.SetFocus
		NoDefault

	Case InList(nKeyCode, 15) && SHIFT + TAB
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.cmdBotoes.btnSair.SetFocus
		NoDefault
EndCase
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE MouseDown
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE MouseDown
LParameters nButton, nShift, nXCoord, nYCoord

Replace Marcas With Not Marcas In crOperacoes
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Refresh()

If crOperacoes.Marcas And !Empty(crOperacoes.Jobs)
	lcJob = crOperacoes.Jobs
	Select EmpDopNums From crOperacoes Where Marcas And Jobs <> lcJob into cursor TmpMarca
	If !Eof([TmpMarca])
		Messagebox([Não é permitido marcar movimentação de jobs diferentes.],48,[Aviso])
		Replace Marcas With Not Marcas In crOperacoes
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Refresh()		
	Endif
	Use in TmpMarca
Endif

NoDefault
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE MouseUp
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE MouseUp
LParameters nButton, nShift, nXCoord, nYCoord

NoDefault
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.pgfComplCots.pgAdicionais.cmdEntrega
* Classe: fwbtnp (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 63
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcEnt

lcEnt = crSigCtTDc.ContaEs

If Not Empty(lcEnt)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [crCompCli], [IClis], lcEnt, [Grupos, IClis, RClis])
	If Not Used([crCompCli])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (cmdEntrega - crCompCli)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In crCompCli
	If Not Eof([crCompCli])
		lcEsc = Iif(crSigCtTDc.ChkAprovs = 0, ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [CONSULTAR])
		
		Do Form SigCdEnd With crCompCli.Grupos, crCompCli.IClis, crCompCli.RClis, ;
							  lcEsc, ThisForm.poDataMgr, crSigCtTDc.LocalEnts

		If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
			Dimension laArray(1)
			laArray(1) = crCompCli.IClis

			If fEndEntrega(@laArray, ThisForm.poDataMgr)
				Replace ContaEs   With laArray(1), ;
						LocalEnts With laArray(02) In crSigCtTDc
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	If Used([crCompCli])
		Use In crCompCli
	EndIf
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

		loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaExt], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdPro], ;
							   [crListaRemota], [CPros], This.Value, [Seleção], 500)

		If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
			loLista.mAddColuna([CPros], [], [Código])

			loLista.mAddColuna([CUnis], [], [Uni])
			loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		
		This.Value = crListaRemota.CPros

		Replace Codigos   With crSigCtTCt.Codigos, ;
				CPros     With crListaRemota.CPros, ;
				DPros     With crListaRemota.DPros, ;
				CUnis	  With crListaRemota.CUnis, ;
				cIdChaves With fUniqueIds() In crSigCtTCt
		Use In crListaRemota
	EndIf
	This.Refresh
EndIf

	loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaExt], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdUni], ;
						   [crListaRemota], [CUnis], This.Value, [Seleção], .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna([CUnis], [], [Uni])
		loLista.mAddColuna([DUnis], [], [Descrição])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	
	This.Value = crListaRemota.CUnis
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
This.Refresh

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcQuery = [Select CUnis ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPro ] + ;
			   [Where CPros = '] + crSigCtTCt.CPros + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalPro]) < 1)
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalPro)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In LocalPro
	If Not Eof([LocalPro]) And Not Empty(LocalPro.CUnis) And (LocalPro.CUnis <> This.Value)
		=MessageBox([A Unidade de Medida Deste Produto No Cadastro é "] + LocalPro.CUnis + ["!!!], ;
					0+16, [Atenção!!!])
		Return .f.					
	EndIf
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column5.Header1
* Classe: header (Base: header)
* Registro: 86
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
Local llRet

llRet = .t.
If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
	llRet = MessageBox([Deseja Realizar a Seleção Automática de Valores?], 4+32+256, [Atenção!!!]) = 6
EndIf

If llRet
	Select crSigCtTCt
	Scan
		For I = 1 To 6
			lcCol = Padl(I, 2, [0])
			If Not Empty(crSigCtTCt.Tota&lcCol.) And (Empty(crSigCtTCt.ValAdqs) Or (crSigCtTCt.ValAdqs > crSigCtTCt.Tota&lcCol.))
				Replace Marca01 With .f., ;
						Marca02 With .f., ;
						Marca03 With .f., ;
						Marca04 With .f., ;
						Marca05 With .f., ;
						Marca06 With .f. In crSigCtTCt

				Replace ValAdqs 	 With crSigCtTCt.Tota&lcCol., ;
						Marca&lcCol. With .t. In crSigCtTCt
			EndIf
		Next
	EndScan
	Go Top In crSigCtTCt
Else
	Return .f.
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If (This.Value < 0)
	This.Value = 0
	This.Refresh
EndIf

lnUni = crSigCtTCt.Tota05 / Iif(crSigCtTCt.QtdAdqs=0, 1, crSigCtTCt.QtdAdqs)

If (crSigCtTCt.Unit05 <> lnUni)
	Replace Unit05 With lnUni In crSigCtTCt
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column21.Header1
* Classe: header (Base: header)
* Registro: 118
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE DblClick
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE DblClick
If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
	Update crSigCtTCt Set Marca06 = .t. Where Not Empty(Tota06)
	Update crSigCtTCt Set ValAdqs = Tota06 Where Marca06

	Update crSigCtTCt ;
	   Set Marca01 = .f., ;
	   	   Marca02 = .f., ;
	   	   Marca03 = .f., ;
	   	   Marca04 = .f., ;
	   	   Marca05 = .f.
	Go Top In crSigCtTCt
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Refresh 
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column21.Check1
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 119
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
NoDefault
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LParameters nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

If InList(nKeyCode, 13, 32)
	If crSigCtTCt.Marca06
		Replace Marca06 With .f., ;
				ValAdqs With 0 In crSigCtTCt
	Else
		Replace Marca01 With .f., ;
				Marca02 With .f., ;
				Marca03 With .f., ;
				Marca04 With .f., ;
				Marca05 With .f., ;
				Marca06 With .t., ;
				ValAdqs With crSigCtTCt.Tota06 In crSigCtTCt
	EndIf
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Refresh
	NoDefault
Else
	Do Case
		Case InList(nKeyCode, 9) && TAB
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.getDPros.SetFocus 
			NoDefault

		Case InList(nKeyCode, 15) && SHIFT + TAB
			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdFornecs.Column2.Text1.SetFocus
			NoDefault
	EndCase
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE MouseDown
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE MouseDown
Lparameters nButton, nShift, nXCoord, nYCoord

If crSigCtTCt.Marca06
	Replace Marca06 With .f., ;
			ValAdqs With 0 In crSigCtTCt
Else
	Replace Marca01 With .f., ;
			Marca02 With .f., ;
			Marca03 With .f., ;
			Marca04 With .f., ;
			Marca05 With .f., ;
			Marca06 With .t., ;
			ValAdqs With crSigCtTCt.Tota06 In crSigCtTCt
EndIf
ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Refresh
NoDefault
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE MouseUp
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE MouseUp
LParameters nButton, nShift, nXCoord, nYCoord

NoDefault
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) And (crSigCtTDc.ChkAprovs = 0) 
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdFornecs.Column3.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 132
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LParameters nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

Do Case
	Case InList(nKeyCode, 9) && TAB
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column1.Text1.SetFocus
		NoDefault

	Case InList(nKeyCode, 15) && SHIFT + TAB
		ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.cmbPrioris.SetFocus 
		NoDefault
EndCase
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

If Padr(this.Tag,40) = Padr(this.Value,40)
	Return .t.
Endif

With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdFornecs
	If Not Empty(This.Value)
		loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaExt], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdCli], ;
							   [crListaRemota], [RClis], This.Value, [Seleção], 1000)

		If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
			loLista.mAddColuna([RClis],   [], [Nome])
			loLista.mAddColuna([IClis],   [], [Código])
			loLista.mAddColuna([Contato], [], [Contato])
			loLista.mAddColuna([Tel1s],   [], [Telefone])
			loLista.Show()
		EndIf
		
		If (Lastkey() <> 27)
			.Column2.Text1.Value = crListaRemota.IClis
*!*				If Empty(crSigCtTFn.Fornecs)
				Replace Fornecs With crListaRemota.RClis In crSigCtTFn
*!*				EndIf
*!*				If Empty(crSigCtTFn.Contatos)
				Replace Contatos With crListaRemota.Contato In crSigCtTFn
*!*				EndIf
*!*				If Empty(crSigCtTFn.Tel1s)
				Replace Tel1s With crListaRemota.Tel1s In crSigCtTFn
*!*				EndIf
*!*				If Empty(crSigCtTFn.Fretes)
				Replace Fretes With [Emitente] In crSigCtTFn
*!*				EndIf
		Else
			Replace Iclis With [], Fornecs With [], Contatos With [], Tel1s With [],  Fretes With [] In crSigCtTFn		
		EndIf
		Use In crListaRemota
	Else
		Replace Iclis With [], Fornecs With [], Contatos With [], Tel1s With [],  Fretes With [] In crSigCtTFn	
	EndIf
	.Column1.Text1.Refresh 
	.Column2.Text1.Refresh 
	.Column3.Text1.Refresh 
EndWith
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
this.Tag = Padr(this.Value,40)
Return InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) And (crSigCtTDc.ChkAprovs = 0) 
ENDPROC

This.Refresh

		loLista.mAddColuna([NComps],   [], [Nome])

		loLista.Show()

	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
This.Refresh

	Replace AprSols	With Solicitas In crSigCtTDc

* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 157
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

Declare laFil[1,2]
laFil[1,1] = [CAtivos]
laFil[1,2] = [S]

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaExt], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdUsu], ;
						   [crListaRemota], [Usuarios], This.Value, [Seleção], .t., [Usuarios], @laFil)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna([Usuarios], [XXXXXXXXXXX], [Solicitante])
		loLista.mAddColuna([NComps],   [], [Nome])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	
	This.Value = Iif(Lastkey()=27, [], crListaRemota.Usuarios)
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
This.Refresh
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR]) And (crSigCtTDc.ChkAprovs = 0) 
ENDPROC

================================================================================

* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 176
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnSair.Click
Local llRet

llRet = .t.
If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
	llRet = MessageBox([Deseja Realmente Abandonar as Alterações?], 4+32+256, [Atenção!!!]) = 6
EndIf

If llRet
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.RollBack()

	With ThisForm.PgfPagina
		.pgFiltros.Enabled  = .f.
		.pgDados.Enabled    = .t.
		.pgCotacoes.Enabled = .f.
		.pgPedidos.Enabled  = .f.
		
		.ActivePage = 2
		.Refresh

		.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Column1.SetFocus
	EndWith
Else
	ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.cmdGravar.btnGravaDados.SetFocus 
	Return .f.
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.cmdGravar
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 177
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnGravaDados.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnGravaDados.Click
If Not ThisForm.GravaDados()
	Return .f.
EndIf

ThisForm.Processamento

* Voltando Para a Página de Dados
With ThisForm.PgfPagina
	.pgFiltros.Enabled  = .f.
	.pgDados.Enabled    = .t.
	.pgCotacoes.Enabled = .f.
	.pgPedidos.Enabled  = .f.
	
	.ActivePage = 2
	.Refresh

	.pgDados.grdOperacoes.Column1.SetFocus
EndWith
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE btnGeraPedidos.Click

PROCEDURE btnGeraPedidos.Click
If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
*!*		If Not ThisForm.loObj.LibSolics And (IsEmpty(crSigCtTDc.DtSolics) Or Empty(crSigCtTDc.Solicitas))
*!*			=MessageBox([A Cotação Ainda Não Passou Pela Aprovação do Solicitante!!!] + Chr(13) + ;
*!*						[Não é Possível Gerar Um Pedido à Partir Dela!!!], 0+16, [Atenção!!!])
*!*			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.pgfComplCots.ActivePage = 2
*!*			ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.pgfComplCots.pgSolicitas.getAprSols.SetFocus
*!*			Return .f.
*!*		EndIf

*!*		If IsEmpty(crSigCtTDc.DtAprovs) Or Empty(crSigCtTDc.Aprovador) Or (crSigCtTDc.ChkAprovs <> 1)
*!*			=MessageBox([A Cotação Ainda Não Passou Pela Aprovação Final!!!] + Chr(13) + ;
*!*						[Não é Possível Gerar Um Pedido à Partir Dela!!!], 0+16, [Atenção!!!])

	If Not ThisForm.GravaDados()
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	ThisForm.ConsultaPedidos
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnInserir.Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnInserir.Valid
Local lnItn

lnItn = 1
Select crSigCtTCt
Scan
	Replace CItens With lnItn In crSigCtTCt
	If Empty(crSigCtTCt.CPros)
		Delete In crSigCtTCt
	Else
		lnItn = lnItn + 1
	EndIf
EndScan

Insert Into crSigCtTCt (Codigos, CItens, CPros, DPros, Obs, Qtds, CUnis, QtdAdqs, cIdChaves) ;
				Values (crSigCtTDc.Codigos, lnItn, [], [], [], 0, [], 0, fUniqueIds())

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column1.SetFocus 
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE btnExcluir.Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE btnExcluir.Valid
Local lnItn

Delete In crSigCtTCt

lnItn = 1
Select crSigCtTCt
Scan
	Replace CItens With lnItn In crSigCtTCt
	If Empty(crSigCtTCt.CPros)
		Delete In crSigCtTCt
	Else
		lnItn = lnItn + 1
	EndIf
EndScan

Insert Into crSigCtTCt (Codigos, CItens, CPros, DPros, Obs, Qtds, CUnis, QtdAdqs, cIdChaves) ;
				Values (crSigCtTDc.Codigos, lnItn, [], [], [], 0, [], 0, fUniqueIds())

ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgCotacoes.grdCotacoes.Column1.SetFocus 
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

		NoDefault
EndCase
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao.getContaDs
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 229
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
With ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao
	If Not Empty(This.Value)
		If Not fAcessoContas(Usuar, .getGrupoDs.Value, [C], .getContaDs.Value, .getContaDs, .getDContaDs)
			=MessageBox([Acesso Negado!!!], 0+48, [Atenção!!!])
			.getContaDs.Value  = []
			.getDContaDs.Value = []
		EndIf
	Else
		.getContaDs.Value  = []
		.getDContaDs.Value = []
	EndIf
	.getContaDs.Refresh
	.getDContaDs.Refresh
EndWith
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Not Empty(ThisForm.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao.getGrupoDs.Value) And InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao.cmdEntrega
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 233
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmdEntrega.Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmdEntrega.Valid
Local lcEnt

lcEnt = crSigCtTDc.ContaEs

If Not Empty(lcEnt)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [crCompCli], [IClis], lcEnt, [Grupos, IClis, RClis])
	If Not Used([crCompCli])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (cmdEntrega - crCompCli)])
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Go Top In crCompCli
	If Not Eof([crCompCli])
		Do Form SigCdEnd With crCompCli.Grupos, crCompCli.IClis, crCompCli.RClis, ;
							  ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, ThisForm.poDataMgr, crSigCtTDc.LocalEnts

		If InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
			Dimension laArray(1)
			laArray(1) = crCompCli.IClis

			If fEndEntrega(@laArray, ThisForm.poDataMgr)
				Replace ContaEs   With laArray(1), ;
						LocalEnts With laArray(02) In crSigCtTDc
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	If Used([crCompCli])
		Use In crCompCli
	EndIf
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao.getDopes
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 238
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loPed

loPed = ThisForm.PgfPagina.pgPedidos

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	If Not Seek(Padr(This.Value, 20), [crSubniveis], [Dopes])
		loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaInt], [crSubniveis], [Dopes], Padr(This.Value, 20), [Operações], .t.)
		loLista.mAddColuna([Dopes], [], [Operação])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf

	If (Lastkey()=27)
		loPed.cntOperacao.getDopes.Value = []
	Else
		loPed.cntOperacao.getDopes.Value    = crSubniveis.Dopes
		loPed.cntOperacao.getCMoes.Value    = crSubniveis.CMoes
		loPed.cntOperacao.getGrupoDs.Value  = crSubniveis.GruDests
		loPed.cntOperacao.getContaDs.Value  = crSubniveis.ConDests
		loPed.cntOperacao.getDContaDs.Value = []
	EndIf
EndIf
loPed.cntOperacao.getDopes.Refresh
loPed.cntOperacao.getCMoes.Refresh
loPed.cntOperacao.getGrupoDs.Refresh
loPed.cntOperacao.getContaDs.Refresh

If Not Empty(loPed.cntOperacao.getContaDs.Value)
	ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery([SigCdCli], [LocalCli], [IClis], loPed.cntOperacao.getContaDs.Value, [RClis])
	If Not Used([LocalCli])
		=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (getDopes - CursorQuery - LocalCli - 1)])
		oTop.Complete
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	loPed.cntOperacao.getDContaDs.Value = LocalCli.RClis
	loPed.cntOperacao.getDContaDs.Refresh
EndIf
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGMVCOT.pgfPagina.pgPedidos.cntOperacao.getCMoes
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 239
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loLista

If Not Empty(This.Value)
	loLista = CreateObject([fwBuscaExt], ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, [SigCdMoe], ;
						   [crListaRemota], [CMoes], This.Value, [Seleção], .t.)

	If Not loLista.plAchouRegistro
		loLista.mAddColuna([CMoes], [], [Cod])
		loLista.mAddColuna([DMoes], [], [Descrição])
		loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Value = Iif(Lastkey()=27, [], crListaRemota.CMoes)
	Use In crListaRemota
EndIf
This.Refresh
* Fim **************************************************************************************
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return InList(ThisForm.loObj.pcEscolha, [INSERIR], [ALTERAR])
ENDPROC
